का . प्रा . 723. -- भारतीय मानक ब्यूरो ( प्रमाणन मुहर ) विनियम , 1981 के नियम 7 उपनियम ( ख ) के अनुसरण में 
भारतीय मानक ब्यूरो एतद्द्वारा अधिसूचित करता है कि नीचे अनुसूची में जिन भारतीय मानकों के संशोधन दए गए हैं , वे उक्त 
नियम के द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों के अधीन जारी किए गए हैं । 


अनुसूची 


क्र . 
संख्या 


संशोधित भारतीय मानक की 

संख्या

भारतीय मानक की स्थापना की 
अधिसूचना छपी थी उसकी संख्या

का . पा . 730 : -- भारतीय मानक ब्यूरो ( प्रमाणन मुहर ) नियम 1987 के नियम 7 उनियम ( ख ) के अनुसरण में 
भारतीय मानक ब्यूरो एतद्वारा अधिसूचित करता है कि नोचे अनुसूची में जिन भारतीय मानकों के संगोबत दिए गए हैं, वे उस 
नियम के द्वारा प्रपस शक्तियों के अधीन जारी किए गए है । 

अनुसूची 
कम संशोधिरा भारतीय मानक की संख्या और जिम राजपत्र अधिसूचना में भारतीय संशोधन की संख्या संशोधन लाग होने को

The Central Government has reteried the following Jo 
custrial Dispute to this Tribunal under section 1071 ) ( d ) 
of the Inclustiul Disputes Act for adjudication , 
" Whether the action of the Sub- Divisional Officer 

( Telegraph ) Khamgaon Akolu is justified in re 
Ironchin Shiri Kashinatlı Haribhan Raut , casual 
workman wie. f 23- 1 - 1988 is justified ? If not, to 

what relief the workman concerned is entitled to i " 
2 . The workmin at whose instance the reference is made, 
did not turn un hefore the Tribunal, alhough he has been 

vily served and about half a dozen of subsequent remindere 
have been issuoj to him . The management has opposed 
the claim by filing a written statedient, fi absence of 
Workman s statement of claim it is not necessary to rcfer 
to its details . In absence of any materials on record . I 
hold that the iction of the Sub- Divisional Officer ( Tele 
graphs) , Khamu . Akola is not shown to justified . 
Workman Kashinath Harihahu Raut is not entitled to any 
clief. Nu orier il to costs . Award accordingly .

JUDGEMENT 


This is an application by the Indian Telephone Indus 
tries Limited , Bangalore , under section 33 ( 2 ) ( b ) of the 
industrial Disputes Act, 1947, for approval of teir action 
of dismissing the opponent workman for habitual absence 
from dury without permission . 


2 . The application arises out of Reference No . NTE 
of 1984 . The workman was charge - sheeted for the mis 
conduct of habitual absence from duty without permission . 
The enquiry was conducted by a committee consisting of 
Shri D . Nagcsha Rao, Office Superintendent, Telephone 
Division . The workman did not appear before the 
committee and as such the proceedings went on ex -parte . 
Witnesses were cxarnined in support of the charge . The 
enquiry committee held the charge of habitual absence 
rfom duty without permission , duly proved . Tile Discip 
linary Authority after considering the proceedings and 
findings of the enquiry committee , passed a speaking 
order dismising the wokman with effect from 1 - 12 - 1989. 
Requirements of the proviso o section 33 ( 2 ) ( b ) have been 
duly complied with . On these averments the Manage 
ment seek ar proval of their action . 

3 . The worloga is duly served with notice by hind 
delivery . He has chosen to remain absent. Shri K . G . S . 
Alva , Personnel Manager, has filed an affidavit in support 
of the averments in the application . “ I have also gone 
through the enquiry procecdings . I am satisficd that the 
action of the Managernent is in order . Accordingly , I 
allow the aprlication and grant th : requisite approval to 
the Manager nt s action under Section 33 ( 2 ) ( b ) of the 
Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 . There will be no order as 
to costs . 


2 . Th : workman remained absent during the ocurse of 
the departmental coquiry as well as boîore this Tribunal. 
As his service could not be secured by the management 
at his last known address , my learned predecessor , justice 
Jandar, directed substituted service by public publication 
in local newspapers. 112 Service on thc workman is 
hold valid . In the departmental proceedings also , service 
by publication in newsrapers had to be resorted to .

ORDER 


ORDER 


This is an application under section 33 ( 2 ) ( b ) oi the In 
dustrial Disputes Act by the Management for apporval of 
their action of terminatig the services of their workman for 
the proved misconduct of committing theft of their property 
and loitering. 


This is an application under section 33 ( 2 ) (b ) of the 
Industrial Disputes Act by the Management for approval of 
their action of terminating the services of their workman 
for the proved misconduct of committing theft of their 
property . 


2 . The workman has been duly served . He has chosen to 
remain absent without filing any written statement. Shri G . 
( . Krishnakumar, Assistant Personnel Manager , has filed his 
affidavit in support of the anplication . On going through 
this affidavit anal the record of the departmental proceedings, 
I am satisfied that the workman was dismissed aiter hold 
ing a fair enquiry in which he was given full oportunity to 
cross -examine the witness of the management an defend 
himself. The charge against him stands substantiitel hy 
more than adequate evidence . The punishment imposed 
upon him is in the circumstances of the case justified . The 
requirements of the proviso to section 33 ( 2 ) ( h ) ibid are 
duly complied with 


2 . The workmin has been duly served . He has chosen 
to remain absent without filing any written statement. Shri 
Abdul Sattar, Assistant Labour Officer has filed his affida 
vit in support of the application . On going through this 
affidavit and the record of the departmental proceedings , I 
am satisfied that the workman was dismissed after holding 
a fair enquiry in which he was given full oportunity to cross 
examine the witnesses of the management and defend him 
self . The charge against him stands substantiated more them 
adequate evidence The punishment imposed upon him in 
in the circumstances of the case justified . The requirements 
of the proviso to section 33 ( 2 ) (b ) ibid are duly control 
with . 


3 . In the recit. I allow the application and approve the 
action of the maurgement in dismissing the workman from 
service . No oritur as to costs. 


3. In the result. I allow the application and anpiove the 
action of the management in dismissing the workmen from 
service . No order as to costs .
